# 16.0 CBR Messages

<a id="MSG"></a>

CBR0001I

**CBR0001I** **OAM** **initialization** **starting.** Explana**tion: The o**bject access method (OAM) control task has received control. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM p**rocessing continues.

CBR0002I

**CBR0002I** **OAM** **initialization** **completed.** Explana**tion: Objec**t access method (OAM) has successfully completed its initialization. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM p**rocessing continues.

CBR0003I

an

**CBR0003I** **Invalid** **option** **specified** **with** **OSMC=** **keyword.** **Parameters** specifi**ed = parm**s**.** Init**i**al**ization termin**a**ted.** Explana**tion: The O**SMC= startup keyword was specified with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to start object access method (OAM) An incorrect option was specified following the OSMC= startup keyword. In the message text: parms ***Th***e specified parameters. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Speci**fy the OSMC= keyword as either OSMC=YES or OSMC=NO.

CBR0005I as

**CBR0005I** **Invalid** **name** **specified** **with** **APLAN=** **keyword.** **Parameters** **specified** = parms**.** Init**i**al**ization termin**a**ted.** Explana**tion: The A**PLAN= startup keyword was specified with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to start object access ' method (OAM). Following the APLAN= keyword is the name of the DB2 plan used by OAM to access the optical configuration database. The DB2 plan name specified with the APLAN= keyword was less than one character or greater than eight characters. In the message text: parms ***Th***e specified parameters. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Verif**y the length of the name of the DB2 plan specified with the APLAN= keyword with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC used to invoke OAM. The name should be from one to eight characters in length.

CBR0006D ends

**an CB**R**000**6**D DB2 sub**s**ys**t**em** I**D not su**p**pl**i**ed b**y **SMS. S**p**ecif**y **S**S**ID or** **rep**ly 'C' to canc**el** **OAM.** Explana**tion: The n**ame of the DB2 subsystem used to gain access is a required during object access method (OAM) start-up. This value is usually provided by the storage management subsystem (SMS), which gets it from the DB2SSID keyword specified in the parmlib member IEFSSNxx for SMS. The DB2SSID keyword was not specified, so the SSID is not available for OAM use. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM w**aits for an operator response. Operato**r Respon**s**e: If yo**u know the DB2 subsystem ID, provide it in the response to the message; the ID must be from one to four characters in length. OAM uses the ID to establish a connection to ' DB2. If this fails, message CBR0010I is issued. If you do not know the ID, reply 'C' to the message. OAM initialization abnormally ends. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: This** message will be issued during / each OAM start-up until the parmlib member IEFSSNxx is updated to include the DB2SSID keyword.

CBR0007I be

**CBR0007I** **Name** **of** **OAM** **DB2** **plan** **not** **specified.** **Initialization** **terminated.** Explana**tion: The A**PLAN= startup keyword was not specified with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to start object access / method (OAM). The APLAN= startup keyword must be specified with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to start OAM. Following the APLAN= keyword should be the name of the DB2 plan used by OAM to access the optical configuration database. The name of the DB2 plan must be from one to eight characters in length. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Speci**fy the name of the DB2 plan using the APLAN= keyword with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to invoke OAM. The name should be one to eight characters in length.

CBR0008I by

**CBR0008I** **OAM** **is** **already** **active** **on** **this** **system.** **Initialization** **terminated.** Explanation: Object access method (OAM) has already been started on this system. Only one OAM address space can be active at a time. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: There** is no need to start OAM again because it is already active. If you are attempting to re-start OAM, wait until the previous invocation of OAM is stopped before attempting / to bring OAM up again. Message CBR0099I will be issued when the previous invocation of OAM has stopped.

CBR0009I are

**CBR0009I** **Unable** **to** **load** **user** **interface** **module.** **Initialization** **terminated.** Explana**tion: The O**bject access method (OAM) control task was unable to load the user interface module. This module should reside in the link pack area (LPA). Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Verif**y that OAM was correctly installed.

CBR0010I continues

**CBR0010I** **Unable** **to** **connect** **to** **the** **Optical** **Configuration** **Data** **Base,** **RC** rc. Initi**a**l**ization termin**a**ted.** Explana**tion: The o**bject access method (OAM) control task was unable to connect to the optical configuration database. The OAM task called OAM to connect to the optical configuration database, but OAM returned a nonzero return code, indicating a failure during the connect. In the message text: rc The return code, to be used for internal diagnostic purposes. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Verif**y the following: OAM optical configuration database was correctly defined and initialized. The correct DB2 subsystem name was specified with the SSID keyword with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to invoke OAM. The correct DB2 plan name was specified with the PLAN keyword with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to invoke OAM.

The OAM task called OAM to disconnect from the optical configuration database, but OAM returned a nonzero return code, indicating a failure during the disconnect. CBR0020I

**CBR0020I** **Error** **during** **CTC** **initialization.** **Initialization** **terminated.** Explana**tion: An er**ror occurred during the CTC initialization phase of object access method (OAM) initialization. This message is preceded by other messages indicating the cause of the error. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Respo**nd as indicated by the programmer response section for the preceding messages.

CBR0021I be

**CBR0021I** **UCB** **not** **found** **for** **CTC** devnum**.** **Initialization** **terminated.** Explana**tion: An er**ror occurred during the CTC initialization phase of object access method (OAM) initialization. The optical configuration database contained an entry for an optical disk drive or an optical disk library, indicating the device was at a access channel-to-channel adapter address. OAM did not find an MVS Unit Control Block (UCB) for the specified device number. In the message text: devnum ***The*** device number. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Verif**y that: All device numbers specified as the CTC device number in the drive table in the optical configuration database are indeed devices that have been defined to the MVS operating system. All device numbers specified as the CTC device number in the library table in the optical configuration database are indeed devices that have been defined to the MVS operating system.

CBR0022I for

**/ CBR**0**022**I **UC**B **for d**evice de**vnum** **doe**s **not ind**i**cate** **th**a**t** i**t** **is** a **CTC de**vice. Initial**ization termin**a**ted.** Explana**tion: An er**ror occurred during the CTC initialization phase of object access method (OAM) initialization. The optical configuration database contained an entry for an optical disk drive or an optical disk library, indicating the device was at a a channel-to-channel adapter address. OAM found an MVS Unit Control / Block (UCB) for the specified device number, but the UCB did not indicate that the device was a channel-to-channel adapter. In the message text: devnum ***The*** device number. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Verif**y that: All device numbers specified as the CTC device number in the drive table in the optical configuration database are indeed devices that have been defined to the MVS operating system as channel-to-channel adapters. All device numbers specified as the CTC device number in the library table in the optical configuration database are indeed devices that have been defined to the MVS operating system as channel-to-channel adapters.

CBR0023I a

**CBR0023I** **Storage** **unavailable** **for** **CTC** **work** **area.** **Initialization** termina**ted.** Explana**tion: An er**ror occurred during the CTC initialization phase of Object access method initialization. For each / channel-to-channel adapter device used by Object access method, a CTC work area is obtained using the STORAGE macro from subpool 241 and anchored to the MVS Unit Control Block. The STORAGE for one of the CTC work areas failed. This message is preceded by message CBR7004I, which indicates the failing return code from the STORAGE macro. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Deter**mine the cause of the STORAGE error by investigating the return code from the STORAGE macro and referring to the documentation for message CBR7004I.

CBR0024I access

**CBR0024I** **Storage** **unavailable** **for** **CTC** **list.** **Initialization** **terminated.** Explana**tion: An er**ror occurred during the CTC initialization phase of object access method (OAM) initialization. During CTC initialization, the OAM constructs a list of all the unique CTC devices it uses. The STORAGE for an area of virtual storage in which to construct the CTC list failed. This message is preceded by message CBR7004I, which indicates the failing return code from the STORAGE macro. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Deter**mine the cause of the STORAGE error by investigating the return code from the STORAGE macro and referring to the documentation for message CBR7004I.

CBR0026I an

**CBR0026I** **Invalid** **option** **specified** **with** **MAXS=** **keyword.** **Parameters** specifi**ed = parm**s**.** Init**i**al**ization termin**a**ted.** Explana**tion: The M**AXS= startup keyword was specified with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to start object access method (OAM). An incorrect value was specified following the MAXS= / startup keyword. The MAXS= keyword must either be omitted, in which case a default of two will be used, or specify a one or two digit numeric value. In the message text: parms ***Th***e specified parameters. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Corre**ct the parameters.

CBR0027I be

**CBR0027I** **SMS** **is** **not** **active** **on** **this** **system.** **Initialization** **terminated.** Explana**tion: The s**torage management subsystem (SMS) is not active on the system where object access method (OAM) startup has been requested. OAM cannot operate without SMS. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Use t**he SET SMS operator command to start SMS, then start OAM again.

CBR0028I

<a id="SPTO0028I"></a>

COMMAND **CBR0028I** **Error** **pinning** **UCB** **at** **address** address **for** **device** device-number**.** be Return **code =** **rc,** R**e**aso**n** **code =** **reas**. Explana**tion: The O**AM control task attempt to "pin" the unit ' control block (UCB) at address address for device device-number using the UCBPIN service. The request was unsucessful. For diagnotic purposes, rc and reas are the return code and reason code, respectively, from the UCBPIN service. In the message text: / ***ad***dress Address at which attempt to pin the UCB is made. device-***number*** The device on which the attempt to pin the UCB is made. rc The return code. reas ***T***he reason code. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. The system sends messages about the job to one of the following: The primary console The remote console, for a system with remote consoles ' The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support (MCS) ' **Ope**r**ator Resp**onse: Repeat the OAM start-up procedure. If the failure persists, notify the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: For i**nformation on UCBPIN return codes and reason codes, see MVS/ESA Component Diagnosis and Logic: Input/Output Supervisor. Contact an IBM programming service representative for assistance. If the error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem reporting databases for a fix for the problem. If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.

CBR0075I are

**CBR0075I** **Unable** **to** **establish** **a** **cross** **memory** **environment.** **Initialization** termina**ted.** Explana**tion: The c**ontrol task attempted to establish a cross memory environment by issuing the MVS macros ATSET, ETCRE and ETCO. One of the macros failed. A preceding message contains the return code from the macro that failed. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Deter**mine the cause of the error by investigating the return code from the macro which failed.

CBR0080I by

**CBR0080I** **Error** **establishing** **the** **{Operator** **Command** **Task** **|** **Library** **control** command ' **t**a**sk |** D**rive co**n**trol** **t**a**sk** | **OAM St**o**rage Manag**e**ment Comp**o**nent T**ask}. Initial**ization termin**a**ted.** Explana**tion: Durin**g the initialization phase of processing, the control task attempted to establish one of the major subtasks. The major subtasks are: Operator Command Task Library control task Drive control task OAM Storage Management Component Task The control task was unable to establish the subtask because the ATTACH of the subtask failed or the subtask did not initialize successfully. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Eithe**r the ATTACH failed or the subtask initialization failed. If the ATTACH failed, this message will be preceded by message CBR7000I, which contains the return code from the ATTACH macro. If the subtask initialization failed, this message will be preceded by messages which further describe that failure. Refer to preceding messages.

CBR0081I bases

AND **CBR0095E** **OAM** **waiting** **for** **SMS** **Control** **Data** **Set** **activation.** Explana**tion: Objec**t access method (OAM) has initialized with a null configuration. When the definitions are completed, activate the control data set (CDS).

CBR0096I

**CBR0096I** **OAM** **restart** **in** **progress.** Explanation: The storage management subsystem (SMS) has activated a new control data set (CDS); the optical configuration may have changed. The object access method (OAM) control task has begun the process of replacing the current optical configuration with the new one. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **All c**urrently active requests are allowed to complete. Requests which are currently queued and waiting for processing, and requests which are received while the restart is in progress, are canceled with the reason code that indicates that the OAM address space is not available. These requests may be reentered when the restart is complete.

Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM r**eceives and processes all user requests.

Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. The system sends messages about the job to one of the following: The primary console The remote console, for a system with remote consoles / The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support (MCS) Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: For i**nformation on the SMS return codes and reason codes see MVS/ESA Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis Reference. If the error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem. If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.

**CBR0181I** **Invalid** **library** **name** libname **specified** **for** **volume** volser**.** Explana**tion: The l**ibrary name specified for the volume is not defined in the SMS ACDS or the definition of the library contained errors. In the message text: libname ***The*** library name. volser ***The*** volume serial number. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **The v**olume is not added to the optical configuration. OAM initialization continues. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: If th**e library name in the volume definition is in error, correct the library name in the volume table in the DB2 optical configuration database, using SPUFI. If the library definition is missing from the SMS CDS, add the definition using the ISMF Storage Administrator library define panel. If the library definition is in error, follow the instructions for the message describing that error. If a new configuration is being activated, and if the volume is not to be part of that configuration, no action is necessary.

CBR0190I

**CBR0190I** **Volume/Slot** **inconsistent.**an ------- S**LOT TAB**L**E --**-**----** -**------** V**OLUME T**A**BLE --**-**----** b**ases** / **LI**BR**ARY** **SLT** VO**L0** VO**L1** VO**L0 LI**BR**ARY** S**LT O** VOL1 bases lib1 `st1` vol1 `v`o`l2` vo`l3` li`b2` `st2` o vol`4` REASONS errors Explana**tion: The i**nformation in the volume table is inconsistent with the information in the slot table. This message contains selected information from the volume table and the slot table. The following fields are displayed: lib1 *L*ibrary name from the row of the slot table in the optical configuration database. st1 Slot name from the row in the slot table in the optical configuration database. vol1 *V*olume serial number of the optical disk volume that

should be at orientation 0 in slot st1 in library lib1. vol2 *V*olume serial number of the optical disk volume that should be at orientation 1 in slot st1 in library lib1. vol3 *V*olume serial number from the row in the volume table. lib2 *L*ibrary that should contain vol3. st2 Name of the slot in library lib2 that should contain volume vol3. o Orientation of volume vol3 in slot st2 in library lib2. vol4 *V*olume serial number of the volume on the opposite side of the optical disk media containing volume vol3. errors *Rea*sons why the slot table is inconsistent with the volume table: 1. Slot table indicates that volume vol1 resides in library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 0, but there is no row in the volume table for volume vol1. This error can be the result of a previously detected error in the definition of volume vol1, as indicated by message CBR0181I or message CBR0182I. 2. Slot table indicates that volume vol1 resides in library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 0, but the library name lib2 associated with volume vol1 in the volume table does not match the library name lib1 in the slot table. 3. Slot table indicates that volume vol1 resides in library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 0, but the slot name st2 associated with volume vol1 in the volume table does not match the slot name st1 in the slot table. 4. Slot table indicates that volume vol1 resides in library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 0, but the orientation o associated with volume vol1 in the volume table indicates it resides in orientation 1. 5. Slot table indicates that volume vol2 resides in library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 1, but there is no row in the volume table for volume vol2. This error can be the result of a previously detected error in the definition of volume vol2, as indicated by message CBR0181I or message CBR0182I. 6. Slot table indicates that volume vol2 resides in library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 1, but the library name lib2 associated with volume vol2 in the volume table does not match the library name lib1 in the slot table. 7. Slot table indicates that volume vol2 resides in library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 1, but the slot name st2 associated with volume vol2 in the volume table does not match the slot name st1 in the slot table. 8. Slot table indicates that volume vol2 resides in library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 1, but the orientation o associated with volume vol2 in the volume table indicates it resides in orientation 0. 9. Volume table indicates that volume vol3 resides in library lib2 in slot st2 However, the entry in the slot table for the same slot in the same library indicates that the slot is not occupied. 10. Volume table indicates that volume vol3 resides in library lib2 in slot st2 in orientation 0. However, the entry in the slot table for the specified slot in the specified library indicates that the volume at orientation 0 is vol1, which is different than volume vol3. 11. Volume table indicates that volume vol3 resides in library lib2 in slot st2 in orientation 1. However, the entry in the slot table for the specified slot in the specified library indicates that the volume at orientation 1 is vol2, which is different than volume vol3. 12. Volume table indicates that volume vol3 resides in library lib2 in slot st2 in orientation 0 and that the volume on the other side of the cartridge is vol4. However, the entry in the slot table for the specified slot in the specified library indicates that the volume at orientation 1 is vol2, which is different than volume vol4. 13. Volume table indicates that volume vol3 resides in library lib2 in slot st2 in orientation 1 and that the volume on the other side of the cartridge is vol4. However, the entry in the slot table for the specified slot in the specified library indicates that the volume at orientation 0 is vol1, which is different than volume vol4. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Make** the appropriate corrections in the slot table or the volume table in the DB2 optical configuration database using SPUFI.

CBR0195I

**CBR0195I** **Volume** **table** **inconsistent.**an ------ VO**LUME T**A**BLE --**-**--- -**-**---- V**O**LUME T**A**BLE --**-**----** a**n** / VO**L0** VO**L1 LI**BR**ARY** **SLT** VO**L0** VO**L1 LI**BRA**RY** SLT an / vo`l1` vo`l2` lib1 `st1` vo`l3` vo`l4` lib2 st2 REASONS reasons END OF DI`SPL`A`Y` Explana**tion: The i**nformation in the volume table for one optical disk volume is inconsistent with information in the volume table for another optical disk volume. This message contains selected information from the volume table for the two optical disk volumes. The following fields are displayed: vol1 *V*olume serial number of the optical disk volume. vol2 *V*olume serial number of the optical disk volume that should be on the opposite side of vol1. lib1 *L*ibrary name of the library that contains volume vol1. st1 Slot name of the slot that contains volume vol1. vol3 *V*olume serial number of the optical disk volume that should be on the opposite side of vol1. This volume serial number should be the same as vol2. vol4 *V*olume serial number of the optical disk volume that should be on the opposite side of vol3. This volume serial number should be the same as vol1. lib2 *L*ibrary name of the library that contains volume vol3. This library name should be the same as lib1. st2 Slot name of the slot that contains volume vol3. This slot name should be the same as st1. reasons *Reas*ons why the volume table is inconsistent: 1. Volume table indicates that volume vol1 resides in library lib1 in slot st1. The opposite side volume is vol2. However, there is no row in the volume table for volume vol2. 2. Volume table indicates that volume vol1 resides in library lib1 in slot st1. The opposite side volume is vol2. However, the row in the volume table for volume vol2 indicates that the opposite side of volume vol2 is vol4, which is different than vol1. 3. Volume table indicates that volume vol1 resides in library lib1 in slot st1. The opposite side volume is vol2. However, the row in the volume table for volume vol2 indicates that volume vol2 resides in library lib2, which is different than lib1. 4. Volume table indicates that volume vol1 resides in library lib1 in slot st1. The opposite side volume is vol2. However, the row in the volume table for volume vol2 indicates that volume vol2 resides in slot st2, which is different than st1. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM i**nitialization stops. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Make** the appropriate corrections in the volume table in the DB2 optical configuration database using SPUFI.

**CBR1010I** **OAM** verb **command** **execution** **failed.** Explana**tion: The o**perator entered a command of the form: F OAM,verb,op`e`r`and(s)` An abnormal end has occurred during processing of the command by the OAM Operator Command Task. In the message text: verb ***T***he command verb entered by the operator. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **The c**ommand can not be processed, depending on when the error occurred. OAM attempts to continue processing in degraded mode. The system sends messages about the job to one of the following: The primary console The remote console, for a system with remote consoles / The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support (MCS) Operato**r Respon**s**e: Do no**t attempt to reenter the failing command until OAM has been stopped and restarted. Schedule an OAM restart at the earliest convenient time. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Notif**y the service representative. If the error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem. If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.

CBR1800I

DATA **CBR1800I** resname **VARY** **completion** **notification** **error.** **SSI** **RC** **=** ssirc**,** **SMS** an RC = sm**sr**c**,** SMS R**E**A**SON** **= smsr**s**n**cd. Explana**tion: Follo**wing completion of VARY command processing for an optical library or an optical drive, object access method (OAM) / tried to notify the Storage Management Address Space (SMAS) using the Subsystem Interface (SSI). The SSI call failed. In the message text: resname ***The*** name of the optical library or the optical drive. ssirc ***Th***e SSI return code. smsrc ***Th***e storage management subsystem (SMS) return code. smsrsnc***d The S***MS reason code. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM c**ontinues normal processing. If a system initial program load (IPL) is performed, the online/offline status of the library or drive can not be correct following the IPL. The system sends messages about the job to one of the following: The primary console The remote console, for a system with remote consoles / The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support (MCS) Operato**r Respon**s**e: Repea**t the failing VARY command. If the failure persists, notify the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: For i**nformation on the SMS return ' codes and reason codes see MVS/DFP Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis Reference. If the error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem. If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.

CBR2000I

**CBR2000I** **Volume** volser **marked** **unwritable.** Explana**tion: Three** consecutive attempts to write on optical an volume have failed with a permanent error on the disk. Object access method (OAM) marked the volume unwritable in the optical configuration database. In the message text: volser ***The*** volume serial number. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM a**ttempts to retry the failing request on another volume. Any future request to write on the unwritable volume fails; a request to read an object that was previously written on the volume is allowed.

CBR2001I configuration

**CBR2001I** **Volumes** volser-1 **and** volser-2 **not** **found** **in** **library** libname**.** Explana**tion: Objec**t access method (OAM) attempted to mount a library-resident optical volume to read or write a data object on the volume. The library slot where the volume resides, according to information in the optical configuration database, is empty or contains a different volume. This error is probably the result of improper manual movement of library volumes. In the message text: volser-***1*** volser-***2 The v***olume serial numbers. libname ***The*** library name. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM m**arks the volumes lost. If the current request / is non-specific, an attempt is made to locate another suitable volume. If no other volume is found, or if the current request is for the specific volume, OAM fails the request. Any future specific request for either volume fails. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Deter**mine where the volumes are actually located. Stop the OAM address space. Using interactive SPUFI services, update the volume table and slot table entries in the optical configuration database to present the correct information. Start the OAM address space.

CBR2002I data

**CBR2002I** **Cross-memory** **copy** **error** **between** **OAM** **address** **space** **and** **ASID** asid**.** Explana**tion: A use**r has requested the writing of a data object to an optical volume or the reading of a data object from an optical volume. An error occurred during the attempt to copy either data or control information cross-memory between user the address space and the OAM address space. In the message text: asid ***T***he address space identifier. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM c**ancels the user request. Request completion is not signaled to the user address space, since the likely result is another cross-memory failure. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: This** is a probable user error. This error can follow the premature end of the user address space, or the premature end of the task in the user address space which requested OAM services, or the premature release of the storage containing the buffer from which the data object is to be written or into which the data object is to be read.

CBR2211E

CONFIGU**RATION** **CBR2211E** **Enter** **unlabeled** **optical** **disks** **into** **library** libname **to** **relieve** **the** are out of **spa**c**e** c**ondit**i**on in sto**r**ag**e **group** s**trgrp**nm. Explana**tion: A req**uest has been made to write a data object on a volume which resides in a library. All volumes residing in the library and belonging to the requested storage group are full or are currently in use, and there are no scratch volumes in the library. This message requests the operator to prepare scratch volumes for use in the library. c.cc 6 In the message text: libname ***The*** library name. strgrpn***m The s***torage group name. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **If sp**ace is available on a volume in another library, and if the request is eligible to use that library, the write operation is processed normally. If space is available on a volume that is currently in use, the write request waits until the volume becomes available. If no space is available on a drive for which the request is eligible, the request fails. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Inser**t an unlabeled optical disk into the library input/output station. Be prepared to supply volume label information for the two volumes on the disk. Message CBR2211E is an action message which is removed from the console when the first unlabeled disk has been successfully entered into the library. If possible, insert several unlabeled disks into the library; consult your system programmer.

**/ CBR**2**212**E **Us**e **th**e **OAM** L**ABEL co**m**ma**n**d to** l**abel op**t**ical** d**isk**s **for** s**hel**f **u**se to code relieve **the ou**t **of** **spa**c**e** c**ondit**i**on in sto**r**ag**e **group** s**trgrp**nm. CBR3910I

**CBR3910I** **There** **is** **no** **online** **and** **operational** **library.** Explana**tion: Durin**g OAM initialization, none of the libraries came up online and operational, the last library online was varied offline, or the last operational library was marked not operational. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System Action: No library requests will be honored until a library is both **onlin**e **and op**erational. Operato**r Respon**s**e: If a** library is offline but operational, issue the SMS VARY command to vary the library online. If a library is not operational and online, issue the SMS VARY command to vary the library offline and then online. If a library is nonoperational and offline, issue the SMS VARY command to vary the library online. If the operational status does not change by varying the library on and offline, contact the service representative.

CBR6001I

AS **CBR6001I** **Unexpected** **drive** **task** **termination** **for** **drive** drvename**.** Explana**tion: The t**ask which manages the optical drive has failed or ended prematurely. In the message text: drvenam***e The d***rive name. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **If OA**M initialization has completed, OAM creates a ' new drive task to manage the optical drive. If a unit of work was active on the drive when the task failed, the unit of work is canceled. If OAM initialization has not yet completed, no attempt is made to create a new drive task. The optical drive is marked not operational and can not be used until OAM has been abnormally ended and restarted. The system sends messages about the job to one of the following: The primary console The remote console, for a system with remote consoles / The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support (MCS) Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: Notif**y the service representative. If the error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem. If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.

CBR6100I data

**CBR6100I** **Cross-memory** **copy** **error** **between** **OAM** **address** **space** **and** **ASID** asid**.** Explana**tion: A use**r has requested the writing of a data object to an optical volume or the reading of a data object from an optical volume. An error occurred during the attempt to copy either data or control information cross-memory between the user address space and the OAM address space. In the message text: asid ***T***he address space identifier. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM c**ancels the user request. Request completion is not signaled to the user address space because the likely result is another cross-memory failure. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer. System **Progra**m**mer Respon**s**e: This** is a probable user error. This error can follow the premature abnormal end of the user address space, the premature abnormal end of the task in the user address space which requested OAM services, or the premature release of the storage containing the buffer from which the data object is to be written or into which the data object is to be read.

CBR6200I error

**/ CBR**6**200I** E**rror wr**i**ting op**t**ical** **VTOC** b**loc**k **for o**bject ob**j**n**ame, ad**dres**s** lba, volume **volser**, drive d**rvena**me. Explana**tion: A use**r has requested that a data object objname be written to optical volume volser on optical drive drvename. Object access method (OAM) encountered an error during the attempt to write the optical volume table of contents (VTOC) to record the location of the data object. In the message text: objname ***The*** object name. lba The approximate physical block address that could not be written. volser ***The*** volume serial number. drvenam***e The d***rive name. System **Action**: **OAM w**ill attempt to take the appropriate action to complete the write request. If the write request cannot be successfully completed, OAM fails the user's write request. Operato**r Respon**s**e: This** message is preceded by a hardware-related error message of the form CBR5nnnI or CBR3110I. Follow the instructions given in the description of that message.

If the message is contained in the message CSECT, verify that the message is coded correctly, especially the message number. If the message is not contained in the message CSECT, check to see if the message is contained in the documentation. If the message is not contained in the documentation, determine the module that is attempting to issue the message. Determine under which conditions the message is being issued and what variable data is to be placed in the message.

**CBR7100I** **Abnormal** **termination** ffsssuuu **in** **task** taskname tcbaddr **at** locatio**n addres**s. If the characters UNKNOWN appear / for address, no system diagnostic work area (SDWA) was provided to the ESTAI recovery routine so the address of the abnormal end could not be placed in the message. In the message text: ffsssuu***u The t***ype of abnormal end, where ff is the indicator flags, sss is the system completion code and uuu is the user completion code. tasknam***e The t***ask that is abnormally ended. tcbaddr ***The*** address of the task control block (TCB) for the abnormally ending task. address ***The*** location of the abnormal end. CBR9917I catalog

**CBR9917I** ctcname modname **optical** **volume** **data** **request** **failed** **for** **volume** volser. Retu**r**n **code i**s **rc.** Explana**tion: The c**ontrol task module tried to get data about an optical volume from object access method (OAM) and failed. The return code identifies the failure. In the message text: ctcname ***The*** name of the control task. modname ***The*** name of the module. volser ***The*** volume serial number. rc The return code is as follows: Return Code (hex) Explana**tion** 1 The volume was not found in the object access method (OAM) Optical Volume Configuration Table. 2 The volume was found in the OAM Optical Volume Configuration Table, but some or all of the data was in error. 3 The STORAGE for the internal copy of the the OAM Optical Volume Configuration Table entry for the volume failed. 4 DB2 failed while trying to get the OAM Optical Volume Configuration Table entry for the volume. Source: **Objec**t access method (OAM) System **Action**: **OAM S**torage Management Component (OSMC) control / task stops when DB2 failed. Otherwise, OSMC abends with completion code X'16' and reason code X'5FFF'. Operato**r Respon**s**e: Notif**y the system programmer.
